<!-- heading-path: Title 17 - ZONING* -->
## Title 17 - ZONING*

<!-- heading-path: Title 17 - ZONING* > Chapter 17.04 - PURPOSE AND TITLE -->
## Chapter 17.04 - PURPOSE AND TITLE

<!-- heading-path: Title 17 - ZONING* > 17.04.010 - Authority, purpose and title. -->
## 17.04.010 - Authority, purpose and title.
A.
Authority. In accordance with authority granted to the city by Title 15, Article 6,
of the Wyoming Statutes, 1977, and amendments thereto, the city has established this Title 17 to the city's code for the promotion of the health, safety, and general welfare of
the community in accordance with the comprehensive plan.
B.
Purpose. The purpose of this title is to lessen congestion in the streets; secure
safety from fire and other danger; promote health and general welfare; provide adequate
light and air; prevent the over-crowding of land; avoid undue concentration of population;
facilitate adequate provisions for transportation, water, sewerage, schools, parks,
and other public requirements; and, foster preservation of historical sites. All reports
and studies which have been adopted as policy by the council shall be applied to the
district under consideration in the implementation of this document. These regulations
have been made with reasonable consideration, among other things, to the character
of the district and its particular suitability for particular uses, with a view to
conserving and improving the living quality of buildings, and encouraging the most
appropriate use of land throughout the city. It is further the intent of this title
to encourage the use of active and passive solar energy throughout the districts provided
for in this chapter. It is also the intent of this title for the commission to conduct
an annual public hearing on a date near the anniversary of the adoption of this title
for the purpose of receiving public comment on the title. The commission shall then
prepare recommendations for any amendments to the title and present them to city council.
The city council will then conduct a public hearing to consider the proposed amendments
to the title.
C.
Title. This title shall be known, referred to and cited as the zoning ordinance, and
will hereafter be referred to as "title."
(Ord. 15-93 (part), 1993; Ord. 5-91 (part), 1991)

<!-- heading-path: Title 17 - ZONING* > Chapter 17.08 - DEFINITIONS -->
## Chapter 17.08 - DEFINITIONS

<!-- heading-path: Title 17 - ZONING* > 17.08.010 - Definitions. -->
## 17.08.010 - Definitions.
"Abut" means to be contiguous to or immediately adjoining (e.g., two lots with a common
property line are considered to be abutting).
"Access aisles" means a way or means by which vehicles leave and enter or travel through
a parking lot.
"Accessory building" means a subordinate and detached structure, the use of which
is incidental to the use of the principal building on the same lot, parcel, or tract.
Accessory buildings shall include detached garages, noncommercial greenhouses, carports
and garden and/or tool sheds.
"Accessory dwelling unit" means a habitable living unit added to, created within,
or detached from a single-family dwelling unit that provides basic requirements for
living, sleeping, eating, cooking and sanitation. Either the principal dwelling unit
or the accessory dwelling unit must be occupied by an owner of the property, or an
immediate family member of the property owner. An accessory dwelling unit shall not
be subdivided or otherwise segregated in ownership from the principal dwelling unit.
"Accessory living quarters" means living quarters within an accessory building and
which occupy no more than twenty-five percent of the area of a principal building
on the same lot, parcel, or tract, and which are used for the sole purpose of persons
employed on the premises. The following uses are included in this definition: servants'
quarters, manager's quarters, and rectories or parsonages in association with churches.
"Accessory use" means a noncommercial use dependent upon or pertaining to the principal
or main use. Such use must be incidental to the principal or main use; and, (a) constitute
a use which is secondary in significance to the principal or main use; and, (b) be
reasonably related to the main use.
"Administrative officer" means the city manager of the city or the city manager's
designated appointee.
Agriculture, Urban. "Urban agriculture" means orchards, gardens, food animals, and
other agriculture produce produced for consumption by those responsible for maintenance
of the lot.
"Aggrieved party" means a person whose legal rights have been detrimentally affected
by a decision, action or rule of law.
"Alley" means a minor private or public thoroughfare, other than a dedicated half
street, which is less than thirty feet wide, upon which the rear of land or building
lots generally abut, and which affords a secondary means of vehicular access to the
land, buildings or lots.
Alterations, Structural. "Structural alterations" means a major change in the load
bearing or non-load bearing members of the building, such as bearing walls, partitions,
volume beams or girders. Any enlargement in the size or height of a building shall
be construed to be a structural alteration.
Alterations, Substantial. "Substantial alterations" means any change in, or enlargement
of, any building or other improvement regulated by this title that will: (a) upon
completion, effect a change in the use; or, (b) enlarge the floor area thereof of
the structure being altered.
"Amateur radio operator" means an operator who uses a radio and/or communication device
for noncommercial, personal use.
"Ambient light" means the existing light condition surrounding an area. The light
source may be sunlight, or artificial light, or a combination of both.
"Amusement center" means a business, the primary purpose of which is to provide entertainment,
including electronic games, billiard tables or pinball machines, etc.
"Animal boarding and treatment center" means an establishment where animals are admitted
for examination, treatment or care by a doctor of veterinary medicine, and which does
include outside runs and kennels. Boarding for all purposes is allowed.
"Animal hospital or clinic" means an establishment where animals are admitted principally
for short-term treatment, examination or care by a doctor of veterinary medicine.
Boarding is provided only for medical purposes or treatment.
Animals, Food. "Food animals" means poultry, sheep, ducks, geese, rabbits, cattle,
swine and other animals raised for the purpose of food consumption.
Animals, Pleasure. "Pleasure animals" means horses, donkeys, burros and llamas kept
for recreational pastime.
"Animal shelter" means a building or premises in which unwanted or lost animals and
fowl are housed, confined, treated and cared for.
"Antenna" means any structure or device used for the purpose of collecting or transmitting
electromagnetic waves, including but not limited to directional antennas, such as
panels, microwave dishes, and satellite dishes, and omni-directional antennas, such
as whip antennas.
"Apartment" means a suite of two or more rooms in a multiple-family dwelling, or in
any other building excluding a single-family dwelling and suitable for occupancy as
a dwelling unit for one family.
Apartment, Efficiency. "Efficiency apartment" means a room within a multifamily dwelling
providing minimum housing accommodation for occupancy by not more than two persons,
which shall include a private bath and may include kitchen facilities, all of which
shall provide a minimum interior floor area of three hundred square feet.
"Apartment house" means a building, or a portion thereof, designed for a residence
by two or more families of persons living independently of each other in separate
apartments.
"Area of special flood hazard" means the land in the floodplain subject to one percent
or greater chance of flooding in any given year.
Arcade. See Amusement center.
"Assisted living" means a dwelling or roominghouse operated by a person, firm or corporation
engaged in the business of operating a facility for the purpose of letting rooms for
rent and providing meals, personal daily living care and limited nursing care for
persons not related to the owner.
"Automobile park" means an area used as a combined sales and service center for one
or more automobile/truck dealerships and similar compatible uses such as automobile
repair shops, detail work shops, muffler shops, vehicle accessory sales shops, and
motorcycle sales, planned and developed as one site, and including business or commercial
uses as determined compatible by the commission.
"Automobile repair shop" means a place where engine fuels may be purchased and the
following services may be obtained: general repair, engine rebuilding, rebuilding
or reconditioning of motor vehicles; collision service, such as body, frame or fender
straightening and repair; and, painting and undercoating of vehicles.
"Automobile sales area" means an area used for the display, sale, or rental of new
or used automobiles and trucks, where no repair work is done except minor incidentals.
"Automobile service center" means an establishment in which the retail sale of accessories
and services for automobiles are provided as the primary use, including the customary
space and facilities for the installation of such parts and fixtures on or in such
vehicles, but excluding the space for major outside storage. Said automobile service
center may be operated in conjunction with a large retail establishment as an accessory
use.
"Automobile service station" means a structure where any automobile engine fuel is
stored and retailed, kerosene, motor oil, lubricants, or greases are retailed directly
to the public with some full-service provision, and which may include a carwash facility
and sale and provision of minor accessories and services for automobiles.
"Awning" means a movable shelter that can be retracted, folded, or collapsed against
the face of the supporting building.
Bar, Tavern, Cocktail Lounge. "Bar," "tavern," or "cocktail lounge" means any licensed
establishment where any amount of alcoholic beverages is sold for consumption on or
off the premises.
"Base flood" means the flood having a one percent chance of being equaled or exceeded
in any given year.
"Basement" means that portion of a building which is partly or wholly below grade,
but so located that the vertical distance from the average grade to the floor is greater
than the vertical distance from the average grade to the ceiling. A basement shall
not be counted as a story.
"Bed and breakfast" means a service in exchange for payment of providing lodging in
a residential dwelling with breakfast for not more than seven consecutive days.
"Bed and breakfast homestay" means a private, owner-occupied residence with a maximum
of two guest rooms, and which is subordinate and incidental to the main residential
use of the dwelling; and where the length of the stay is limited to seven consecutive
days.
"Bed and breakfast inn" means a service approved as a business, even though the owner
may not live on the premises.
"Block" means an area of land within a platted subdivision that according to the plat
is entirely bounded by streets or a street and railroad right-of-way, natural barrier
or adjacent corporate boundary.
"Block frontage" means all of the property of a given block or any portion thereof
lying adjacent to a public street or highway.
"Boardinghouse" means a building other than a hotel or motel, where for compensation
and by prearrangement for definite periods, lodging, and/or lodging with meals are
provided for three or more persons, but not exceeding twenty persons who reside on
the premises.
"Branch community facilities" means a public, noncommercial use established primarily
for the benefit and service of the population of the community in which it is located.
Examples are libraries and post offices.
"Breezeway" means a roofed, open-sided passageway connecting two buildings, such as
a house and a garage. The roof of a breezeway may be either solid or open-structured.
Attached two buildings with a breezeway shall not make them one building for purposes
of determining setbacks.
"Buffering" means the use of specific measures designed to separate and protect differing
land uses on separate properties. See Appendix C.
"Building" means any structure used or intended for supporting or sheltering any use
or occupancy. When divided by a common wall or walls, it may be deemed two separate
buildings.
Building, Detached. "Detached building" means a building having no common wall or
structure in connection with any other building.
"Building frontage" means the horizontal, linear dimension of that side of a building
that abuts a street, parking area, mall, or other area open to the general public
and that has either a main display window or a public entrance to the building.
"Building height" means the vertical distance above grade, as defined herein, to the
highest point of the coping of a flat roof, or to the deck line of a mansard roof,
or to the average height of the highest gable of a pitched or hipped roof. The measurement
may be taken from the highest adjoining sidewalk or ground surface within a five-foot
horizontal distance of the exterior wall of the building. The height of a stepped
or terraced building is the maximum height of any segment of the building.
"Building line" means the minimum setback line.
Building, Nonconforming. "Nonconforming building" means a building existing at the
time of the adoption of the ordinance codified in this title which fails to comply
with the regulations of this title pertaining to the zone in which the building is
located.
"Building official" means the city manager or the city manager's designated representative.
"Bulk plant" means a storage facility for products refined from petroleum, mineral
resources, or any other combustible substance.
"Canopy" means a permanently roofed shelter covering a sidewalk, driveway, or similar
area. It may be supported entirely by a building or partially or entirely supported
by columns, poles, or braces extended from the ground.
Certificate (Child Caring). "Child caring certificate" means the authority to carry
on and operate a child caring facility issued by the Wyoming Department of Health
and Social Services, or similar state agency, and operating as the agency which issues
certificates, makes inspections (or causes same to be made), enforces the standards,
and handles all administrative details affecting the enforcement of state statutes
relating to child caring facilities.
"Child care center" means any private person, partnership, association or corporation
that is operating a business for profit or otherwise, where sixteen or more children
receive care for part of the day.
"Church" means a building wherein persons regularly assemble for religious worship
and which is maintained and controlled by a religious body organized to sustain public
worship, together with all accessory buildings and uses customarily associated with
such primary purpose. Includes synagogue, temple, mosque, chapel or other such place
for worship and religious activities.
"City manager" means the chief administrative officer of the city, whose duties are
established by Wyoming Statutes and by the city council.
Classroom, Temporary. "Temporary classroom" means a portable building designed to
be moved from one public school site to another and used for classroom purposes. The
building shall not be placed upon a permanent foundation or remain at a given site
for a period exceeding two years without an exception being granted.
"Clinic" means an establishment where patients are admitted for special study and
treatment by two or more licensed physicians and/or dentists and their professional
associates, as distinguished from a medical office established for general consultation
purposes.
"Club" or "lodge" means a building used by a group of people who are members of a
society, association, organization, fraternal organization, etc., for meetings, social
events, and/or operations of the society, association, organization, etc.
"Cluster development" means a development designed as a complete and integrated unit
in which the individual units are concentrated on the portion of the site most suitable
for development, and within which prescribed minimum standards for site area, setbacks,
and the bulk and spacing of buildings may be modified to achieve preservation of open
space areas of twenty percent or more of the total acreage exclusive of parking and
roadways.
"Commercial wireless telecommunication services" means licensed commercial wireless
telecommunication services including cellular, personal communication services (PCS),
specialized mobilized radio (SMR), enhanced specialized mobilized radio (ESMR), paging
and similar services that are marketed to the general public.
"Commission" means the planning and zoning commission of the city.
"Common lot line" means any single lot line that serves as a boundary for two adjoining
lots.
"Common wall" means an unbroken wall shared by two or more separate buildings.
"Comprehensive plan" or "comprehensive land use plan" means the general plan for land
use, transportation, and community facilities prepared and maintained by the commission
and adopted by the council.
"Conditional use" means a use which runs with the land may be allowed as provided
herein subject to certain restrictions or conditions on the location, size and other
performance standards, as may be imposed by the commission or the council, after considering
the effect upon the neighborhood in which it is proposed, including aesthetic considerations
and conformity to the comprehensive plan.
"Conditional use permit" means a legal authorization for a conditional use issued
by the commission, consisting of two parts: (a) a written statement of factual determination
by the commission which justifies the issuance of the permit; and, (b) a written statement
of specific conditions that must be met and maintained in order for the use to be
permitted and continued.
"Condominium" means a separate fee simple estate in an individual air space unit of
a multi-unit property together with an undivided fee simple interest in common elements.
As used in this definition, individual air space unit shall consist of any enclosed
room or rooms occupying all or part of a floor or floors in a building of one or more
floors to be used for residential, professional, commercial, or industrial purposes,
and which has access to a public street. Common elements means the land on which a
building or buildings are located, foundations, structural elements of the building,
such as columns, girders, beams, roofs, halls, corridors, stairs, lobbies, entrances
and exits of such building or buildings, parking areas, storage spaces, installations
of utility services, including all apparatus and appurtenances thereto, and such other
community and commercial facilities as may be provided for in any declaration defining
the elements of the condominium ownership.
"Condominium conversion" means the conversion of residential, commercial, office or
industrial buildings to the condominium form of ownership.
Construction, New. "New construction" means structures for which the "start of construction"
commenced on or after the effective date of the ordinance codified in this title.
Convenience Establishment, High Volume. "High volume convenience establishment" means
an establishment primarily designed for the purpose of providing legally stored vehicle
engine fuels, kerosene or motor oil, and lubricants or grease, on a full- or self-service
basis to the traveling public and commercial haulers, which may include restaurant
facilities, shower facilities, and/or overnight sleeping facilities.
Convenience Establishment, Low Volume. "Low volume convenience establishment" means
a business establishment within a building that is not more than three thousand square
feet, that is designed and intended to serve the frequent trade, and service needs
of the neighborhood population immediately surrounding it. Such establishments may
include neighborhood groceries, drug stores, dairy stores, coin-operated laundries,
dry cleaning establishments, laundry and dry cleaning agencies. Automated teller machines
and recycling machines shall be located within a building and not drive-up facilities.
Automobile service stations, automobile repair shops, and drive-through facilities
are specifically excluded from this definition.
Convenience Establishment, Medium Volume. "Medium volume convenience establishment"
means a retail building with a building area of not more than six thousand square
feet in area. The primary business is selling grocery store related products and gasoline/diesel
fuel. Fast in and out service is the prime feature of these businesses.
Convenience Establishment, Neighborhood. "Neighborhood convenience establishment"
means a small business establishment designed and intended to serve the daily, frequent
trade, or service needs of the neighborhood population immediately surrounding it.
Such establishments may include neighborhood groceries, drug stores, dairy stores,
coin-operated laundries, dry cleaning establishments, laundry and dry cleaning agencies.
Automated teller machine and recycling machine shall be located within a building,
which contains not more than one hundred twenty square feet. Filling stations, repair
garages, and drive-in eating and drinking establishments are specifically excluded
from this definition.
"Council" means the city council of the city of Casper.
"Credit union" means a member-owned, nonprofit, cooperative thrift institution formed
for the purpose of encouraging savings by offering a return using collective moneys
to make loans to members, and providing other member services on a cooperative basis
operated under state and federal laws and regulations.
"Custodial care facility" means a public or privately operated facility providing
custodial care for persons residing by court placement, or being held or detained
awaiting trial or court placement, including, without limitation, correctional and
post-correctional facilities, adult and/or juvenile detention facilities, reentry
centers and correctional transitional housing. A custodial care facility shall not
include "group homes," as defined in the municipal code.
"Dance studio" means a facility, the primary purpose of which is to provide instruction
in the art of dancing, acrobatics, gymnastics, exercise dancing or martial arts.
Day Care, Adult. "Adult day care" means a facility providing for not more than ten
adults over the age of twenty.
"Development" means any man-made change to improved or unimproved real estate including,
but not limited to, buildings or other structures, mining, dredging, filling, grading,
paving, excavation, or drilling operations located within the area of special flood
hazard.
"Digital off premises advertising sign" means a computer programmable, off-premises
sign capable of displaying words, symbols, figures or images that can be electronically
or mechanically changed by remote or automatic means.
"Director" means the director of planning and community development, or his designee.
"Dissolve" means a mode of message transition on an electronic message center sign
or a digital off-premises advertising sign accomplished by varying the light intensity
or pattern, where the first frame/message gradually appears to dissipate and lose
legibility simultaneously with the gradual appearance and legibility of the second
frame/message.
"District" means a section (or sections) of the city which is governed by uniform
regulations.
"Domestic servant" means a person employed by another person to do household duties
such as a maid, a cook, a butler, a housekeeper, etc.
"Drive-in" means a business establishment so designed that a portion of its retail
or service character is dependent on providing a driveway approach for motor vehicles,
to serve patrons while in the motor vehicle, rather than from within the building.
"Drive-through facility" means a business establishment or that portion of a business
establishment designed so that motor vehicles may form a line, in order for patrons
to receive services while in motor vehicles, rather than within the building.
"Driveway" means that area on private property where vehicles are operated, parked,
or allowed to stand.
"Dry lot" means a corral or pen used as an enclosure for the keeping of horses, cattle,
swine, poultry, or other animals in a designated area in which feed and water is provided
for the same.
Duplex. See Apartment House.
"Dwell time" means the interval of time that an electronic message center sign or
a digital off-premises advertising sign frame remains static before transitioning
to a new message.
"Dwelling unit" means a complete, independent living, sleeping, eating, cooking, and
sanitation facility for one family.
"Easement" means a right of one person to use in some way the land of another for
a special purpose.
"Electronic message center sign (EMC)" means an on-premises, changeable-copy (automatic)
sign which uses light emitting display technologies or a combination of lights, or
lighted or unlighted panels which are controlled electronically to produce words,
symbols, pictures or messages. Includes cathode ray, light emitting diode (LED) display,
plasma screen, liquid crystal display (LCD), fiber optic, video boards and other similar
electronic technology.
"Enforcement officer" means the city manager or his/her designated appointee.
"Exception" means a modification of the general provisions of this title under particular
given circumstances after it is determined that strict compliance with this title
would cause undue hardship on the applicant.
"Existing manufactured home (mobile) park" or "Existing manufactured home (mobile)
subdivision" means a manufactured home (mobile) park for which the construction of
facilities for serving the lot on which the manufactured homes (mobile) are to be
affixed (including at a minimum, the installation of utilities, either final site
grading or the pouring of concrete pads, and the construction of streets) are completed
before the effective date of the ordinance codified in this title.
"Facility" means a building, structure, etc. that facilitates or houses some particular
activity; or, improvements such as water and sewer connections provided to service
some activity or use.
"Fade" means a mode of message transmission on an electronic message center sign or
digital off-premises advertising sign accomplished by varying the light intensity,
where the first message/frame gradually reduces intensity to the point of not being
legible (i.e. fading to black) and the subsequent message/frame gradually increases
intensity to the point of legibility.
"Family" means one or more persons occupying as a single housekeeping unit and using
common cooking and sanitary facilities, as distinguished from a group occupying a
hotel, club, or boarding house.
"Family child care center - zoning review" means a child care facility in which care
is provided for not more than fifteen unrelated children for part of a day. A family
child care center shall be the principle residence of the provider when such a facility
is located in a residential zoning district. A conditional use hearing with the planning
and zoning commission is required in residential districts.
"Family child care home" means a child care facility in which care is provided for
three to eight unrelated children from more than one immediate family for part of
a day in the home of the provider.
"Family child care home - zoning review" means a child care facility in which care
is provided for nine or ten unrelated children from more than one immediate family
for part of a day in the home of the provider, where a conditional use hearing with
the planning and zoning commission is required in residential districts.
Family, Immediate. "Immediate family" means husband, wife, child, stepchild, brother,
sister, stepbrother, stepsister, parent, stepparent, grandchild, grandparent, mother-in-law,
father-in-law.
"Fence" means a partition, structure, wall, or gate erected as a dividing marker,
barrier, or enclosure.
"Fixture" means a chattel attached to realty and which may not be removed without
doing substantial damage to the realty.
"Flagpole" in reference to a "flag lot," means a strip of land, a minimum of twenty-four
feet in width, connecting the widest area of a "flag lot" to the public right-of-way,
and may be used as the means of access. The minimum width of a flagpole for single-unit,
residential properties in the R-1 (Residential Estate) and R-2 (One Unit Residential)
Zoning Districts, for lots platted prior to January 1,2023, is twelve feet. The minimum
width of a flagpole for single-unit, residential properties in the R-1 (Residential
Estate) and R-2 (One Unit Residential) Zoning Districts, for lots platted after January
1, 2023, is fifteen feet.
"Flood" or "flooding" means a general and temporary condition of partial or complete
inundation of normally dry areas from: (a) the overflow of inland or tidal waters;
and/or, (b) the unusual and rapid accumulation or runoff of surface waters from any
source.
"Floodplain district" means an area designated on the most current flood insurance
rate map prepared by the Federal Emergency Management Agency.
"Flood insurance rate map (FIRM)" means an official map, on which the Federal Emergency
Management Agency has delineated both the areas of special flood hazards and the risk
premium zone applicable to the city.
"Flood insurance study" means the official report provided by the Federal Emergency
Management Agency that includes flood profiles, the flood boundary and floodway map,
and the water surface elevation of the base flood.
"Floodplain approval" means a request for a review of the city's interpretation of
any provision in the floodplain district or a request for a variance in said floodplain
district.
"Floodway" means the channel of a river or other water course and the adjacent land
areas that must be reserved in order to allow the discharge of base flood without
cumulatively increasing the water surface elevation more than one foot.
Flood Area, Gross. "Gross flood area" means the total floor area designed for tenant
(owner) occupancy and exclusive use, including basements, mezzanines, and upper floors,
if any, expressed in square feet, and measured by the bases of interior walls, excluding
common areas such as stairways, halls, storerooms, mechanical rooms, landings, etc.
Floor, Lowest. "Lowest floor" means the lowest floor of the lowest enclosed area (including
basement). An unfinished or flood-resistant enclosure, usable solely for parking of
vehicles, building access or storage, in an area other than a basement area, is not
considered a building's lowest floor, provided that such enclosure is not built so
as to render the structure in violation of the applicable nonelevation design requirements
of this title.
"Footcandle" means the English unit of measurement for illuminance, which is equal
to one lumen, incident upon an area of one-foot.
"Footing" means that portion of the foundation of a structure which spreads and transmits
loads directly to the soil or piles.
"Fowl" means any of the large domesticated birds used as food (chicken, ducks, turkeys,
etc.).
"Foundation wall" means the structure of brick, stone, concrete, steel, wood, or iron
which transfers the building load to the ground.
"Frame" means a complete, static display screen on an electronic message center sign
or digital off-premises advertising sign.
"Full motion video" means a sign feature with simulated continuous movement created
by the display of a series of images, typically consisting of a recorded actual event
or scene.
"Gambling" means risking any property for gain contingent in whole or in part upon
lot, chance, the operation of a gambling device, or the happening or outcome of an
event, including a sporting event, over which the person taking a risk has no control,
but does not include:
1.
Bona fide contests of skill, speed, strength or endurance in which awards are made
only to entrants or the owners of entries; of contracts;
2.
Bona fide business transactions which are valid under the law of contracts;
3.
Other acts or transactions now or hereafter expressly authorized by law;
4.
Raffles or bingo conducted, or pull tabs sold, by charitable or nonprofit organizations
where the tickets for the raffle or bingo are sold only in this state and the pull
tabs are sold only on the premises owned or occupied by the charitable or nonprofit
organization;
5.
Any game, wager or transaction which is incidental to a bona fide social relationship,
is participated in by natural persons only, and in which no person is participating,
directly or indirectly, in professional gambling; or
6.
Calcutta wagering on contests or events conducted by a bona fide nationally chartered
veterans', religious, charitable, educational or fraternal organization or nonprofit
local, civic or service club organized, or incorporated under the laws of this state;
provided, that:
a.
The contest or event is conducted solely in this state;
b.
Any rules affecting the contest or requirements for participants are clearly posted;
c.
The total prizes or prize money paid out in any one contest or event does not exceed
ninety percent of the total wagers;
d.
A minimum of ten percent of the total wagers on each contest or event does not exceed
ninety percent of the total wagers;
e.
No separate organization or professional person is employed to conduct the contest
or event or assist therein;
f.
The sponsoring organization, before conducting the contest or event, gives thirty
days' written notice of the time and place thereof, to the governing body of the county
or municipality in which it intends to conduct the contest or event, and the governing
body does not pass a resolution objecting thereto;
g.
The sponsoring organization has complied with the relevant sections of the Internal
Revenue Code of 1954, as amended, relating to taxes on wagering.
"Gambling device" means any device, machine, paraphernalia or equipment that is used
or usable in the playing phases of any professional gambling activity, whether that
activity consists of gambling between persons or gambling by a person involving the
playing of a machine.
"Gambling premises" means any building, room enclosure, vehicle, vessel or other place,
whether open or enclosed, used or intended to be used for professional gambling. Any
place where a gambling device is found, is presumed to be intended to be used for
professional gambling.
"Gambling proceed" means all money or property at stake or displayed in, or in connection
with, professional gambling.
"Gambling professional" means:
1.
Aiding or inducing another to engage in gambling, with the intent to derive a profit
therefrom; or
2.
Participating in gambling and having, other than by virtue of skill or luck, a lesser
chance of losing or a greater chance of winning than one or more of the other participants.
"Gambling record" means any record, receipt, ticket, certificate, token, slip or notation
given, made, used or intended to be used in connection with professional gambling.
"Gaming area" means the gross floor area which includes the actual gaming devices
and adjacent areas necessary to use or service the gaming device such as aisleways,
seating and standing areas devoted to gaming device operation.
Gaming/Gambling. For the purpose of this title, "gaming and gambling" shall have the
same meaning and may be used interchangeably.
"Gaming establishment" means an establishment so licensed for gaming or gambling activity
under the laws of the state of Wyoming.
Garage, Private. "Private garage" means an enclosed building, structure, or portion
thereof, that fronts on any street or alley and can be used for the storage of any
vehicle or other personal property for persons living on the premises, and having
no public shop or service in connection therewith. (For apartment houses, each apartment
unit will be permitted a double stall garage or equivalent space in a larger structure.)
Garage, Public. "Public garage" means a place where the following services may be
performed: general repair, engine rebuilding, rebuilding or reconditioning of motor
vehicles, collision service such as body, frame, and fender straightening and repair,
painting and undercoating of automobiles, and the sale of engine fuels legally stored.
"Garage sale" means a noncommercial function held for the purpose of selling household
or personal items such as, but not limited to, tools, furniture, toys, appliances,
and personal belongings, by the residents of the dwelling and lasting a maximum of
three consecutive days.
"Grade" means the ground adjoining the building or structure on all sides.
Grade Level. "Grade level" means the average point of elevation of the finished surface
of the ground, paving, or sidewalk within the area between the building and the property
line.
"Grocery store" means a retail establishment whose primary business (more than fifty
percent of total sales) is selling food or food products, and has a floor area greater
than four thousand square feet.
"Ground anchors" means any device designed for the purpose of securing a manufactured
home (mobile) or other accessory structures to the ground.
"Group home" means an establishment used to provide twenty-four hour care or supervision,
by persons living on the premises who, by reason of circumstances, require daily care
or supervision. "Care" is defined as room and board, and the provision of a planned
treatment program, "Planned treatment" means a previously determined program of counseling,
therapy, or other rehabilitation social service which provides for a group of persons
of similar or compatible circumstances or conditions.
"Health officer" means the administrator of the department of health and medical services,
Wyoming Department of Health and Social Services, or his authorized representative
(city-county health department).
"Historic site" means any structure or site officially approved by the council as
having significance to the city or any site or structure officially designated by
the state or the federal government as having historical significance.
Homes for the Homeless (Shelters). "Homes for the homeless" includes:
a.
Long-Term Shelters. Long-term, nonprofit accommodations for individuals and families
who have been displaced from their homes. For the purpose of this section, long-term
means a period of more than six months, but less than twelve months.
b.
Short-Term Shelters. Nonprofit facilities that provide short-term nightly accommodations
for indigent people without homes. For the purpose of this section, short-term means
for a period of less than six months.
"Hotel" means a building in which lodging is provided and offered to the public for
compensation.
Hospital, Mental. "Mental hospital" means an institution licensed by state or federal
agencies under the provisions of law to offer facilities, care, and treatment for
cases of mental and nervous disorders.
"Illuminance" means the photometric quality most closely associated with the perception
of brightness and a measurement of the intensity of light falling on a surface at
a given distance from the light source. Illuminance can be measured with a footcandle
meter, also known as a luxmeter.
Improvement, Substantial. "Substantial improvement" means any repair, reconstruction,
or improvement of a structure, the cost of which equals or exceeds fifty percent of
the market value of the structure based on the administrative authority or a bona
fide appraisal of the property either before the improvement or repair is started
or, if the structure has been damaged and is being restored immediately, before the
damage occurred.
"Junkyard" means a place in excess of two hundred square feet, where waste discarded
or salvaged materials are bought, sold, exchanged, baled, packed, disassembled, handled,
or stored, including auto wrecking yards, house wrecking yards, used lumber yards,
and places or yards for storage of salvaged house wrecking and structural steel material
and equipment; but not including places where such uses are conducted entirely within
a completely enclosed building.
Kennel, Commercial. "Commercial kennel" means any lot or premises on which more than
three dogs, cats, or other pets are kept, boarded, bred, or sold, and which may provide
outdoor animal pens and runs.
"Landscaping" means the use of forms of planting and vegetation or nonliving inorganic
durable materials identified below, to enhance the visual attractiveness of a site
and improve erosion control. Landscaping shall include any of the following:
a.
Formal turf areas;
b.
Trees, shrubs, bushes, groundcover, or planting;
c.
Sprinkler systems;
d.
Decorative rock, natural or man-made;
e.
Gardens, exposed aggregate, tile, or similar decorative materials used in walkways,
excluding sidewalks on public property, driveway approaches, and any architectural
features attached to the building;
f.
Decorative lighting;
g.
Benches, tables, fountains, waterfalls and man-made streams, planters, kiosks, and
public transit shelters;
h.
Decorative fences and retaining walls, i.e., railroad ties, flagstone;
i.
Ponds, excluding detention and retention ponds as defined in this chapter.
"License" means a permission accorded by a competent authority conferring the right
to do or forbid from doing some act, which without such authorization would or would
not be allowed, illegal, or would be a trespass or a tort.
"Lighted recreational facility" means any outdoor area used as an arena, field, court,
course, etc., which is lighted and usable for the purposes of recreation during evening
hours.
"Loading space" means an off-street space for the standing, loading, and unloading
of goods, to avoid undue interference with the public use of streets and alleys.
"Lot" means a portion of a subdivision or parcel of land used for building development.
"Lot area" means the total area of a lot on a horizontal plane bounded by lot lines.
Lot, Corner. "Corner lot" means a lot situated at the intersection of two or more
intersecting streets with a boundary thereof bordering each street. A corner lot shall
be presumed to have two frontages. Both front yards shall follow the required setbacks
established for each zoning district.
"Lot depth" means the horizontal distance between the front and rear lot lines, measured
along the median between the two side lot lines.
Lot, Flag. "Flag lot" means a platted parcel of property shaped like a "flag" or "panhandle"
with its widest area set back some distance from a publicly dedicated and improved
street, and having a strip of land ("flagpole") that provides the property with frontage
on a public street.
"Lot lines" means the boundaries/perimeters of a lot. See Figure 17.08.010B.
Lot Line, Front. "Front lot line" means, for an interior lot, that line separating
the lot from the street right-of-way. See Figure 17.08.010B.
Lot Line, Rear. "Rear lot line" means the lot line opposite the front lot line. In
case of an irregular shaped lot, the rear lot line shall be an imaginary line not
less than ten feet in length and parallel to the front lot line. See Figure 17.08.010B.
Lot Line, Side. "Side lot line" means any lot line other than the front or rear lot
line. See Figure 17.08.010D.
Lot Line, Zero. "Zero lot line" means a side or rear lot line which has either no
setback or less than the minimum setback as permitted in the zoning districts. See
Figure 17.08.010C.
Lot, Through. "Through lot" means a lot having two frontages on parallel or approximately
parallel streets.
"Lot width" means a straight line distance between the side lot lines at the front
property line.
"Major watercourse" means a natural stream of water fed from permanent or periodical
nature sources and usually flowing in a particular direction in a defined channel.
See Figure 17.08.010E.
"Manufactured home" means a single-family residential structure transportable in one
or more sections, which when assembled equals or exceeds twenty-four feet in width,
and thirty-six feet in length. Such structure is designed to be attached to a permanent
foundation, was constructed after January 15, 1976, and is built in compliance with
either the Manufactured Home Construction and Safety Standards of the U.S. Department
of Housing and Urban Development (HUD), or the Uniform Building Code, as adopted.
Manufactured Home (Mobile), Certified. "Certified Manufactured (mobile) home" means
a structure, transportable in one or more sections, which equal or exceeds eight feet
in width or thirty-two feet in length, and is built on a permanent chassis and designed
to be used with or without a permanent foundation when connected to the required utilities,
and is constructed in accordance with the Manufactured Home (Mobile) Construction
and Safety Standards of the U.S. Department of Housing and Urban Development, or if
constructed prior to June 15, 1976, is built in accordance with ANSI Standards, Bulletin
#1.119.1 as amended.
"Manufactured home (mobile) park expansion" means the preparation of additional sites
by the construction of facilities for servicing lots on which manufactured homes (mobile)
are to be affixed (including the installation of utilities, either final site grading
or pouring of concrete pads, or the construction of streets).
"Manufactured home (mobile) park" means a parcel or contiguous parcels of land divided
into two or more manufactured home (mobile) spaces for rent or lease.
Manufactured Home (Mobile) Park, Existing. "Existing manufactured home (mobile) park"
means a manufactured home (mobile) park for which the construction of facilities for
servicing the lot on which the manufactured home (mobile) is to be affixed (including,
at a minimum, the installation of utilities, either final site grading or the pouring
of concrete pads, and the construction of streets), and which is completed before
the effective date of the ordinance codified in this title.
"Mobile home contractor" means a person who may conduct, carry on, or engage in the
business of connecting, blocking, leveling, skirting, and all other above ground requirements
necessary to place a manufactured home (mobile) in established parking areas.
Mobile Home, Independent. "Independent mobile home" means a manufactured home (mobile)
which has both flush toilet and a bath or shower.
"Mobile home installer" means a person who labors at the trade, or receives compensation
in any form for connecting the gas, waste, and/or water lines in a manufactured home
(mobile) as an employee of a manufactured home (mobile) contractor.
"Modular home" means a residential structure transportable in one or more sections,
which when assembled equals or exceeds twenty-four feet in width, and thirty-six feet
in length. Such structure, and all additions thereto, are designed to be attached
to a permanent foundation, and are built in compliance with the Uniform Building Code,
as adopted.
"Montessori" means a system of training and teaching young children devised in 1907
by Maria Montessori, which emphasizes training of the senses and aims at self-education
through guiding, rather than controlling the child's activity.
"Motel" means a series of commercial rental units containing bedroom, bathroom, and
closet space which provides overnight lodging for compensation.
"Neighborhood" means contiguous areas showing common characteristics of population
and homogeneity of land use.
"Neighborhood assembly use" means those permitted or conditional land uses defined
within Title 17 of the municipal code which occupy a building or portion of a building in which facilities
are provided for the public or membership groups to assemble for civic, educational,
political, religious, entertainment, or social purposes, with a seating capacity of
fewer than six hundred persons.
"Neighborhood grocery" means a business establishment, the purpose of which is the
retail sale of food or food products with a building area of not more than four thousand
square feet.
"Net acre" means the number of square feet in a natural acre (43,560) less that portion
which is set aside for street, easements, or other public purposes.
"Nonconforming use" means the lawful use of a building, structure or land on the effective
date of the ordinance codified in this title, or amendments thereto, which does not
conform to the provision of this title for the district in which it is located.
"Nursery" means a space or building, or a combination thereof, for cultivation, storage,
or sale of live trees, shrubs, or plants including products used for gardening or
landscaping. The definition of a nursery does not include any space or building used
for the sale of fruits, vegetables, or other related products.
"Nursing home" means any institution, place, building, or agency in which any accommodation
is maintained, furnished, or offered for the hospitalization of the sick or injured,
or care of any persons requiring or receiving chronic or convalescent care, not including
hotels or other similar places that furnish only board and room, or either, to their
guests, nor to hospitals operated by the federal government.
Nursing Home, Intermediate Care. An "intermediate care nursing home" provides a modest
amount of nursing care, as defined by the Wyoming Department of Health and Social
Services, with a nurse on duty twenty-four hours per day, seven days per week. The
nurse shall have a minimum designation of a Practical Classification No. 1. (Practical
Nurse as classified by the state.)
Nursing Home, Skilled Care. A "skilled care nursing home" provides for more than a
modest amount of nursing care, as defined by the Wyoming Department of Health and
Social Services, with a nurse on duty twenty-four hours per day, seven days per week.
This will require a nurse with a registered nurse (RN) designation for the day shift
and a licensed practical nurse (LPN) for the other two shifts. The licensed practical
nurse(s) shall have a Classification No. 1.
Office, General Business. "General business office" means a structure, room, or suite
of rooms in which the clerical and administrative work of a business or service for
the public is performed.
Office, Professional. "Professional office" means a building where there is no display
of stock or wares in trade, nor commodity sold, nor any commercial use conducted other
than those incidental to the services provided. For purposes of this title professional
office shall include the office of a doctor, dentist, lawyer, architect, engineer,
geologist, minister of religion, insurance agent, real estate agent, real estate appraiser,
accountant, or similar professions or professional services.
"Official map" means the official map of the city as adopted by the council.
Open Drainageways. See Stream Channel.
Open Space, Usable. "Usable open space" means unobstructed open space controlled and
maintained by a property owners association for use by the occupants of a planned
unit development, or by others for recreation. The term "useful open space" shall
not include space devoted to buildings, streets, and off-street parking areas.
"Overlay" means to superimpose planned unit development over an existing lot layout
and in a specific zoning district.
"Owner" means the owner of real property who was, in the last calendar year, liable
for a property tax or would have been liable but for legal tax exemption.
Owner, Contract Purchase. "Contract purchase owner" means a purchase in which legal
title to property remains with the seller until the purchaser has fulfilled the terms
of the contract.
"Parking space" means a space required for vehicular parking of a single vehicle,
which must be surfaced with asphalt or concrete, and may vary in size.
"Parkway" means that area of land located between the back of the street curb and
the property line including any sidewalk or landscaping located therein.
"Paving width" means the distance so designated from the front or leading edge of
the curb to the front or leading edge of the curb opposite and not necessarily the
width of the right-of-way.
"Permit" means a written authorization issued by the city pursuant to law.
"Person" means any individual, person, partnership, corporation, association, governmental
corporation or entity.
"Personal service shop" means a business which specializes in providing a service
such as a barbershop, beauty shop, etc. A personal service shop shall not be considered
a professional office for the purpose of this title.
"Pet supply shop" means a business which sells pet accessories, supplies, food, and/or
small pets such as cats, dogs, etc., on a retail or wholesale basis.
"Pets" means poultry or any animal that is tamed or domesticated.
"Pharmacy" means a business where prescription drugs and medicinal aids and products
are sold on a retail basis to the general public.
"Planned treatment" means a previously determined program of counseling, therapy,
or other rehabilitation social service provided for a group of persons of similar
or compatible circumstances or conditions.
"Planned unit development" means a type of land development designed to provide variety
and diversity which may be residential, commercial, industrial, or a mixture of residential/commercial,
commercial/industrial, in which buildings may be clustered and/or set on lots of a
size smaller than normally required by this title. The PUD may result in an overall
density, the same density, or a greater density than is required by this title. The
PUD must contain large, open, park-like areas with buildings concentrated in a few
areas. Individual properties may be owned in fee with open space either owned jointly
or deeded to the city.
"Planning director" means the chief administrator of the planning office, directly
responsible to the city manager.
Plastics, Approved. "Approved plastics" shall be classified as either CC1 or CC2,
in accordance with the Uniform Building Code, the most recent version.
Platted Lot, Individual. "Individual platted lot" means a tract of land shown as a
lot on a subdivision plat, which has been recorded in the office of the county clerk,
in accordance with state statutes.
"Pond, Detention. "Detention pond" means stormwater runoff collected and stored in
a detention pond for a short period of time, so that the outflow from the detention
pond is a reduced dischargeable rate.
Pond, Retention. "Retention pond" means stormwater runoff collected and stored in
a retention pond for an infinite period.
"Preschool" means any public, parochial or private school for children under kindergarten
age.
"Preservation" means retaining without substantial alteration the exterior portion
of an existing structure. A change of the architectural style or scale of the structure
shall be deemed to be a substantial alteration.
"Principal building" means the building or a use that complies with the purpose, permitted
uses and/or conditional uses as defined in the zoning district in which the building
or use is located.
"Private school" means one legally maintained by private individuals or corporations,
not at public expense, and open only to pupils selected and admitted by the proprietors
or governors.
"Property lines" means the legal boundaries of any particularly described parcel of
land.
"Public entity" means a person, actual or artificial, that is recognized by law.
"Public hearing" means an administrative legal proceeding with definite issues of
fact to be determined and/or with the parties having the right to be heard and have
counsel present.
"Public land" means land owned, controlled, and/or operated by a governmental entity.
"Public notice" means notice given to the public generally, or to the entire community,
or to all whom it may concern, or through the use of the local news media, including
a newspaper of general circulation, or the recording of documents.
Publication, Legal. "Legal publication" means an official notice in a newspaper of
general circulation as prescribed by state statutes.
"Raw land value" means unimproved land, land in its natural state prior to grading,
construction, subdividing, or improvements such as streets, lighting, and sewers.
Real Property, Private. "Private real property" means the private interests, benefits
and rights inherent to the ownership of real estate.
Real Property, Public. "Public real property" means the public interests, benefits
and rights inherent to the ownership of real estate.
"Recreation center" means a facility which provides recreational activities, equipment
and facilities such as swimming pools, racquetball courts, weight lifting equipment,
etc., to members of the general public.
"Recreational overnight park" means land occupied or designed for temporary occupancy
by any one of the following: tent, pickup camper, camp trailers, or motor home.
"Recreational vehicle (RV)" means any of the following:
a.
A vehicular, portable structure built on a chassis, designed to be used as a temporary
dwelling for travel, in accordance with ANSI Standards Bulletin No. 119-A.
b.
Pickup camper means a structure designed to be mounted on a truck chassis, for use
as a temporary dwelling for travel, recreation, and vacation.
c.
Motor home means a portable, temporary dwelling, to be used for travel, recreation,
and vacation, constructed as an integral part of a self-propelled vehicle.
d.
Camping trailer means a structure mounted on wheels and designed for travel, recreation,
and vacation use.
"Recreation vehicle parking area" (overnight parks, destination parks, primitive parks,
or campgrounds) means a parcel of land in which two or more spaces are occupied or
intended for occupancy by recreational vehicles for transient dwelling purposes.
"Recreational vehicle sewer connection" means the connection consisting of all pipes,
fittings, and appurtenances from the drain outlet of the recreational vehicle to the
inlet of the corresponding sewer riser pipe of the sewage system serving the recreational
vehicle parking areas.
"Recreational vehicle space" means a parcel of land in a recreational vehicle parking
area for the placement of a single recreational vehicle and the exclusive use of its
occupants and accessory structures.
"Recycling center" means a structure or storage area whose primary use is where waste
or scrap materials are stored, bought, sold, accumulated, exchanged, packaged, disassembled,
or handled, including, but not limited to, scrap aluminum, paper, plastic and bottles
are processed.
"Regional assembly use" means those permitted or conditional land uses defined within Title 17 of the municipal code which occupy a building or portion of a building in which facilities
are provided for the public or membership groups to assemble for civic, educational,
political, religious, entertainment, or social purposes, with a seating capacity of
six hundred persons or more.
"Sanitary station" means a facility provided for the emptying and flushing of hoses
or sewage holding tanks of recreational vehicles.
School, Elementary, Junior, and Senior High. "Elementary school," "junior high school"
and "senior high school" means any public, parochial or private school for any grades,
including kindergarten and/or between first and twelfth, that may or may not be accredited
by the Wyoming Department of Education.
School, Park Concept. "Park concept school" means the siting of a school adjacent
to a public park, creating a combination of open space/recreational uses.
"Scrolling message" means a mode of message transition on an electronic message center
sign or digital off-premises advertising sign where the message appears to move vertically
or horizontally across the display surface. For the purpose of regulating electronic
message center signs and digital off premises advertising signs, scrolling shall refer
to text only, and not images or symbols which shall instead, be considered either
animated signs or full motion video signs.
"Service building" means a building for a manufactured home (mobile) park or other
residential development, housing facilities such as mail facilities, recreation facilities,
toilet and bathing facilities, etc.
"Service riser pipe" means that portion of the sewer lateral which extends vertically
to the ground elevation and terminates at each recreational vehicle space.
"Service sink" means a sink used for cleanup purposes within the service building.
"Setback" means the distance required to comply with the front, side, or rear yard
provisions of this title. See Figure 17.08.010D.
"Short duration" or "temporary" means a period not to exceed thirty calendar days.
"Sign" means any identification, description, illustration, or device, illuminated
or nonilluminated, which is visible from any public place or is located on private
property and exposed to the public, which directs or draws attention to a product,
service, place, activity, person, institution, business, or solicitation including
any permanently installed or situated merchandise; or any emblem, painting, banner,
pennant, or placard designed to advertise, identify, or convey commercial information.
Sign, Accessory. "Accessory sign" means a sign which is accessory to the principal
use of the premises.
Sign, Animated. "Animated sign" means any sign which includes simulated movement,
action or motion created by the display of a series of pictures or images.
Sign, Changeable Copy (Automatic). "Automatic changeable copy sign" means a sign or
electronic message center on which the copy or image changes automatically on a lampbank
or display through a programmed or remote electronic or mechanical means.
Sign, Changeable Copy (Manual). "Manual changeable copy sign" means a sign on which
copy is changed manually in the field.
Sign, Electric. "Electric sign" means any sign containing electric wiring which is
attached or intended to be attached to an electrical energy source.
"Sign face" means the surface of a sign upon, against, or through which the message
is displayed or illustrated.
Sign, Flashing. "Flashing sign" means any sign which contains an intermittent or flashing
light source, or which includes the illusion of intermittent or flashing light, or
where the identical message, or a portion of the message is repeated at a rapid interval
(less than every two seconds) for the purpose of drawing attention to the sign.
Sign, Freestanding. "Freestanding sign" means a sign that is supported by one or more
columns, upright poles, or braces extended from the ground or from an object on the
ground, or that is erected on the ground, where no part of the sign is attached to
any part of a building, structure, or other sign; this term includes pole signs, pedestal
signs, and ground signs. It shall not include portable signs. See Figure 17.08.010F.
"Sign height" means the vertical distance measured from the highest point of the sign
or sign structure to the grade of the nearest adjacent street or to the grade of the
street or highway to which the sign is directed.
Sign, Illegal Nonconforming. "Illegal nonconforming sign" means a sign that was in
violation of any of the laws of the city governing the erection or construction of
such sign at the time of its erection and that has never been brought in conformity
with all such laws.
Sign, Legal Nonconforming. "Legal nonconforming sign" means any sign that was lawfully
erected and maintained prior to the enactment of the ordinance codified in this title,
but that does not conform to all applicable regulations and restrictions contained
herein or any sign located in an area annexed to the city since the enactment of the
ordinance codified in this title that was legal at the time of its construction.
"Sign maintenance" means replacing, repairing, or repainting of a portion of a sign
or sign structure or renewing of a copy that has been made unusable by ordinary wear
and tear, weather, or accident.
Sign, Off-premises Advertising. "Off-premises advertising sign" means a sign including,
but not limited to, billboards or general outdoor advertising devices, that advertise
or direct attention to a business, commodity, service, or activity conducted, sold,
or offered elsewhere than on the same lot or parcel or within the same building upon
which the sign is located.
Sign, On-premises. "On-premises sign" means a sign that advertises a business and
directs attention to a business, commodity, service or activity conducted, sold or
offered on the same lot, parcel or within the same building upon which the sign is
located.
Sign, Projecting. "Projecting sign" means a sign that extends in whole or in part
fifteen inches or more horizontally beyond the surface of the building to which the
sign is attached. See Figure 17.08.010F.
Sign, Real Estate. "Real estate sign" means a sign indicating the availability for
sale, rent, or lease of a specific lot, building, or portion of a building upon which
the sign is erected or displayed.
Sign, Roof. "Roof sign" means any sign erected upon, against, or directly above a
roof or on top of or above the parapet of a building. See Figure 17.08.010F.
Sign, Rotating. "Rotating sign" means any sign or portion of a sign which moves in
a revolving or similar manner.
"Sign structure" means any supports, uprights, braces, or framework of a sign.
Sign, Temporary. "Temporary sign" means any commercial sign, banner, display board,
poster, or other advertising display constructed of paper, plastic, wood, cloth, canvas,
light fabric, cardboard, or other light materials, with or without frames, intended
to be displayed for a period of time not to exceed thirty calendar days. This definition
does not include governmental flags or political signs regulated pursuant to Section 17.96.070 .
Sign, Wall. "Wall sign" means a sign displayed upon or against the wall of an enclosed
building, where the exposed face of the sign is in a plane parallel to the plane of
the wall and extends not more than fifteen inches from the face of the wall. See Figure
17.08.010F.
"Site" means the specific location upon which a building, structure, development,
use, etc., and any accessory use or building, off-street parking areas, or landscaping
is located.
"Site Plan" means a plot plan of the entire area to be developed, which shows location
and size of buildings, the area of the land under consideration, street names and
widths, parking area size, and all other items required on the site plan checklist
(Section 17.12.150 (K)). A site plan is required for all PUD's, all new multi-family residential buildings
consisting of sixteen or more living units, all new public, commercial and industrial
buildings, and churches, and all exterior additions, of one thousand square feet or
larger, to multi-family residential buildings, existing public, commercial and industrial
buildings, and churches.
"Site plan agreement" means an agreement entered into between the owner and the city
for various improvements to be made to the site in accordance with provisions of this
title.
"Sleeping room" means a room containing no kitchen facilities, which may or may not
have a private bath, and which is rented on a permanent or temporary basis.
"Solar collector" means one of the following which is capable of collecting, storing
or transmitting at least twenty-five thousand BTU's on a clear winter solstice day:
a.
A wall, clerestory or skylight window designed to transmit solar energy into a structure
for heating purposes;
b.
A greenhouse attached to another structure and designed to provide part of the heating
load for the structure to which it is attached;
c.
A trombe wall, "drum wall," or other wall or roof structural element designed to collect
and transmit solar energy into a structure;
d.
A photovoltaic collector designed to convert solar energy into electric energy;
e.
A plate-type collector designed to use solar energy to heat air, water, or other fluids
for use in hot water or space heating or for other applications; or
f.
A massive structural element designed to collect solar energy and transmit it to internal
spaces for heating.
"Solar right" means a property right to an unobstructed line-of-sight path from a
solar collector to the sun which permits radiation from the sun to impinge directly
on the solar collector. The extent of the solar right shall be described by the illumination
provided by the path of the sun on the winter solstice day which is put to a beneficial
use or otherwise determined by 1977 Wyoming Statutes Sections 34-22-101 through 34-22-106
as amended.
"Specialty shop" means a business which specializes in the sale of one kind of product
and closely related products such as a bookstore, a fabric store, or a florist shop.
"Spot zoning" means the singling out of a particular property or small groups of properties
for different treatment from that accorded to similar surrounding land; which is contrary
to the general pattern of zoning in the surrounding geographic area and is not in
accordance with the comprehensive plan; and, which is designed solely for the economic
benefit of the owner of the property receiving special treatment.
"Start of construction" includes substantial improvement, and means the date the building
permit was issued, provided the actual start of construction, repair, reconstruction,
placement, or other improvement was within one hundred eighty calendar days of the
permit date. The actual start means the first placement of permanent improvements
on a site, such as the pouring of slab or footings, the installation of piles, the
construction of columns, or any work beyond the stage of excavation or the placement
of a manufactured home on a foundation. Permanent improvements does not include land
preparation, such as clearing, grading, and filling; nor does it include the installation
of streets and/or walkways; nor does it include excavation for a basement, footings,
piers, or foundations or the erection of temporary forms; nor does it include the
installation on the property of accessory buildings, such as garages or sheds not
occupied as dwelling units or not part of the main structure.
"Static message" means a sign message/frame that does not have movement, or the appearance
or optical illusion of movement during the display period.
"Storage" means the act of depositing goods, wares, and/or merchandise in any structure,
or part of a structure.
Storage, Open. "Open storage" means a contained or restricted area used for storing
materials or new and used vehicles not awaiting repair or demolition.
Store, Retail. "Retail store" means a use devoted to the sale of goods and services.
Store, Wholesale. "Wholesale store" means a use devoted to the wholesaling of goods.
"Story" means that part of a building between the surface of a floor and the ceiling
immediately above, but not including a basement.
"Street" means that portion of an easement of ground designated and dedicated to the
public to accommodate a thoroughfare, avenue, road, highway, boulevard, parkway, drive,
circle, court, or lane within the city.
Street, Arterial. "Arterial street" means the main channels for the movement of traffic
in an urban area and are generally wider than local streets and are given preference
in signing and signalization.
"Street frontage" means the linear dimension of that side of a lot or parcel abutting
a public or private street that provides principal access to or visibility of the
premises.
Street, RV Park. "RV park street" means that portion of the RV park so designated
to afford access to, or from, a public way and which is maintained by the RV park
operator.
Street, Private. "Private street" means a portion of a lot, tract, parcel, subdivision,
PUD, or development so designated to afford access to or from a public way and which
is used and maintained by the property owners, for their private use only.
"Street right-of-way" means that portion of ground dedicated to the public for use
of the public for access, recreation, or temporary parking of motor vehicles.
"Structure" means that which is built or constructed, an edifice or building of any
kind, or any piece of work artificially built up or composed of parts joined together
in some definite manner.
"Sundry shop" means a business which sells, on a retail basis, numerous kinds of products
such as a drug store, a variety store, a craft shop, a gift shop, etc.
Tandem Parking Space, Stacking. "Stacking tandem parking space" means a parking space
which provides for the parking of vehicles, one behind the other.
"Temporary" means a period not to exceed thirty calendar days.
"Thrift shop" means a shop which sells new and used merchandise, predominantly used.
All such materials shall be displayed and/or stored in an enclosed building.
"Tower" means any ground, roof or otherwise mounted pole, spire, structure or combination
thereof that is fifteen feet or greater in height above the ground, including supporting
lines, cables, wires, braces, masts or other structures, for the purpose of mounting
an antenna, meteorological device or similar apparatus aboveground.
"Townhouse" means a single-family dwelling unit constructed as part of a series of
two or more single-family dwelling units sharing a common vertical wall and a common
lot line, and which is located on a single, individually owned lot.
"Tract" means a continuous expanse of land under single ownership.
"Trade school" or "business school" means a school conducted as a commercial enterprise
for teaching instrumental music, dancing, barbering, or hairdressing, or for teaching
industrial skills in which machinery is employed as a means of instruction or business
skills. A trade school shall include, but is not limited to, facilities which perform
the necessary lab functions to support career training programs, including facilities
for heavy and mechanical equipment, painting shops, building construction and metal
fabrication/welding shops. This definition shall not be deemed to include school as
defined in this title.
"Trailer van" means a highway or industrial type vehicle designed with or without
wheels to be hauled, pulled as by a tractor or motor vehicle over the highway, and
used for temporary storage.
Trailer Van, Construction. "Construction trailer van" means a trailer van used by
licensed contractors in the construction of buildings, installation of utilities in
new or existing buildings or subdivisions, or on street construction projects.
"Transient sales lot" means any area that is used exclusively for the sale or taking
of orders for any merchandise, where such sales or order taking are not part of the
operation of an established business or where no permanent physical structures or
facilities are used as integral parts of the sales or order taking operation.
"Transition" means the visual effect of changing from one frame/message to another
frame/message on an electronic message center sign or digital off-premises advertising
sign.
"Transitional area" means an area used as a buffer between different intensities of
use or different zoning districts.
"Transportation terminal" or "transportation depot" means a facility specifically
designated for the storage, transfer, or boarding and unloading of material or persons
such as a bus station, a railroad station, etc.
Tree Farm. See Nursery.
"Truck stop" or "car stop" means an establishment primarily designed for the purpose
of providing legally stored vehicle engine fuels, kerosene or motor oil, and lubricants
or grease, on a full and some self-service basis to semi-trucks, which may include
similar services for car/truck stop facilities, restaurant facilities, and/or overnight
sleeping facilities.
"Twin home" means a combination of no more than two single-family dwelling units with
each individual dwelling unit located on its own legal lot, and sharing a common lot
line and a common vertical wall.
"Unit space" means a designated plot of ground within a manufactured home (mobile)
park for the accommodation of one unit.
Use, Permitted. "Permitted use" means a use which is allowed.
"Variance" means a variation or adjustment of the strict application of this title
in the case of an irregular, narrow, shallow, or steep lot or other physical condition
applying to a lot which is not customary to the abutting lots, and would result in
practical difficulty or unnecessary hardship that would deprive the owner of reasonable
use of the land involved.
"Vehicle" means a device in, upon, or by which any person or property may be transported
or drawn upon a highway excluding devices moved by human power or used exclusively
upon rails or tracks, implements of husbandry, machinery used in construction work
not mainly used for the transportation of property over highways, and pedestrian vehicles
while operated by a person who by reason of a physical disability is otherwise unable
to be mobile as a pedestrian.
Vehicle, Compact. "Compact vehicle" means any vehicle with dimensions less than sixteen
feet long, six feet wide, and five feet in height.
Vehicle, Handicapped. "Handicapped vehicle" means any vehicle lawfully displaying
an identification sticker as legally issued for handicapped parking.
Vehicle, Standard. "Standard vehicle" means any vehicle other than a handicapped vehicle
or a compact vehicle.
"Warehouse" means a structure or part of a structure, public or private, for storing
goods, wares, and merchandise, whether for the owner or others.
"Warehousing" means the storing or securing of goods, wares, and merchandise in a
warehouse; any structure or part of a structure.
"Water connection" means the connection consisting of all pipes, fittings, and appurtenances
from the water riser pipe to the water inlet pipe of the distribution system within
the recreational vehicle.
Watercourse, Major. "Major watercourse" means a natural stream of water fed from permanent
or periodical and natural sources and usually flowing in a particular direction in
a defined channel, having a bed or bank and sides, and usually discharging itself
into some other stream or body of water.
Water Supply, Public. "Public water supply" means any water supply being distributed
by ten or more service connections, utilized to furnish water for human consumption
either in preparing foods or beverages for inhabitants of residences or business establishments
or as defined in Wyoming State Statutes, 1977, 35-11-103 (C) (viii), as amended.
"Wholesale" means the process of buying in large quantities from the producer or manufacturer
and selling the same to jobbers or to retail dealers.
Workshop Scale Manufacturing, Assembling, or Packaging of Products from Previously
Prepared Materials. "Workshop scale manufacturing, assembling, or packaging of products
from previously prepared materials" means a facility where previously prepared materials
are modified or repaired using labor intensive techniques as opposed to capital intensive
mass production assembly techniques. Employees within a workshop scale manufacturing
facility are characterized as craftsmen as opposing to factory assembly workers.
"Wrecker service" means the towing of wrecked, damaged or inoperable vehicles from
one location to another location. Such service shall not include the storage or junking
of the towed vehicles.
"Wrecking" means the dismantling or wrecking of motor vehicles or trailers, or the
storage, sale, or dumping of dismantled, obsolete or wrecked vehicles or their parts.
"Wrecking yard" means any establishment or place of business which is maintained or
